======== move-in ========

======== house ========

[firstnight] Bowie, Millie, Hicks, Axel, Zee, Owen, Brightly, Beth @kitchen #st:sn.f2
    ~ First night. Everyone is crowded round the kitchen island, still in the clothes they arrived in.
  Bowie: Okay, can we do the thing where we all say one fact about ourselves?
  Millie: No.
  Hicks: Absolutely not.
  Bowie: Come on, it's an icebreaker!
  Axel: The ice is broken. We broke it. Somebody cried in the bathroom an hour ago.
  Zee: That was me. I'm fine. It's just a lot.
  Millie: It is a lot.
  Bowie: Okay, new icebreaker. Everybody say who they think is going to win.
    ~ Silence. Everybody looks at everybody.
  Hicks: ...Let's go back to facts.
  Bowie (DR): The second I said 'who's going to win', you could see every single person do the maths. That's when it stopped being a party.
    ~ In the middle of it all, Bowie and Millie are no longer laughing.
  Millie: You okay? You've been quiet all day.
  Bowie: Why does everybody keep asking me that?
  Millie: Because you've been quiet all day.
  Bowie: Maybe I just don't want to talk to anybody. Is that allowed?
  Millie: It's allowed.
  Bowie: Great. Then let me do it.
    ~ Millie holds both hands up and backs out of the room.
  Hicks: Can we not do this here? In front of everybody?
    ~ They do it right there anyway.
  Millie (DR): I tried. I'm not trying again. And I'm not going to pretend I'm fine. Bowie knows exactly what she did.
    ~ The music goes off. The lights dim. Somebody laughs in the dark at nothing.
  Hicks (DR): Best night in the house. Probably. Everybody likes everybody tonight. It won't last.

[friction] Scary, Millie @bedroom #st:sf.c3
    ~ Bedroom, after lights out. Scary is talking, loudly.
    ~ Two beds over, Nichelle is asleep. Or says so.
  Millie: Can you keep it down? People are trying to sleep.
  Scary: It's only like eleven, relax.
  Millie: It's half one. Some of us have been up since seven.
  Scary: Oh. Sorry, I didn't realise.
    ~ Two minutes later Scary laughs at something, just as loud as before.
  Millie: I'm not asking again.
  Millie (DR): I've had about four hours of sleep since I got here, and most of the missing ones are Scary's fault. I've got a short fuse, and Scary keeps finding it.

[banter] Bowie, Chase, Ripper, Beth @backyard #st:gb2.2
    ~ Somewhere behind them, Axel shoots a basketball, misses, and swears.
  Chase: What do you think they're watching at home right now?
  Bowie: Us, eating crisps.
  Ripper: Riveting.
  Beth: Hi, everyone. These crisps are very good.
  Bowie: Don't advertise crisps!
  Beth: I'm not advertising, I'm sharing a review.
  Chase: Five stars. Would eat again on national television.
  Ripper: We're all going to look insane.
  Bowie (DR): Somewhere, people are watching four adults review crisps. I hope they're having as much fun as we are.

[latenight] Nichelle @bedroom #st:ll.5
    ~ Everyone else is asleep. Nichelle is lying awake, staring at the ceiling.
  Nichelle (DR): You'd think it would be quiet at night. It's not. A house full of people breathing, a fridge humming, and a camera turning to look at you every time you move.
    ~ Nichelle rolls over. A camera on the wall turns with a soft whirr.
  Nichelle (DR): See? There it goes again. Even when you're alone in here, you're never actually alone.

[prank] Zee, Millie, Bowie, Chase @kitchen #st:gx3.3
    ~ Kitchen. Zee has replaced the sugar with salt. Bowie and Chase know. Millie does not.
  Bowie: Make us all a cup of tea, Millie?
  Millie: Yeah.
  Chase: Two sugars in mine.
  Millie: Coming up.
    ~ Millie takes a big sip of her own tea first. Silence.
  Millie: ...Why does this taste like the sea?
    ~ Zee, Bowie and Chase completely lose it.
  Millie (DR): Three people, one salt pot, and my dignity. I'll get them back. I've got all season.
  Millie (DR): After the fight with Zee, I wanted to hide. Chase came and found me.

[formed] Axel, Nichelle, Brightly @bedroom #st:gf.1
    ~ Bedroom, door shut. Axel has brought them in one at a time: Nichelle, then Brightly, then everyone else.
  Brightly: Okay, why are we all in here? This looks really suspicious.
  Axel: Because I want to say something once, to all of you, so nobody hears it second-hand.
  Nichelle: I'm all ears.
  Axel: Everyone in this room, I trust. Everyone outside it, I don't know yet. So I want us to be a group. Properly.
  Brightly: How many of us?
  Axel: Look around. This many. No more.
  Nichelle: And we vote together?
  Axel: Every time. We argue in here, we vote out there as one.
  Brightly: I'm in.
  Nichelle: In.
  Axel: Then we're The Double Edge.
  Brightly: Who came up with that?
  Axel: Me. Just now. Live with it.
  Brightly (DR): Walked into a bedroom with no idea, walked out in an alliance. That's how fast it happens in here. I don't make promises lightly. Axel just got one.

[island-bonfire] Zee, Brightly, Axel @backyard #isb.p6
    ~ Around the fire on the beach. The marshmallows ran out an hour ago, and the questions got sharper.
  Zee: Never have I ever thought about who I'd put up if I won HOH.
    ~ Everybody drinks. Everybody laughs.
  Brightly: Some of us have thought about it more than others.
  Axel: Meaning who?
  Brightly: Meaning nobody. It's a joke.
  Axel (DR): That wasn't a joke. That was a name with the name left out.

[island-gym] Zee, Brightly @backyard #isg.r4
    ~ The treadmills in the gym. Zee turns the speed up. Brightly, on the next one, turns it up more.
  Zee: You okay there?
  Brightly: Never better.
  Zee: You're red.
  Brightly: You're redder.
  Zee (DR): We ran for forty minutes and neither of us got off first. That's the next endurance comp, right there.

[firstbed] Ripper, Millie, Emmah, Caleb, Hicks, Owen, Beth, Scary @bedroom #st:fb.2
    ~ Lights out, first night. Somebody is whispering a list of everyone’s names, trying to remember them.
  Ripper: Millie, Emmah, Hicks... who's next to Hicks?
  Caleb: Me. It's Caleb.
  Ripper: Caleb. Sorry, it's been a long day.
  Millie: Are you doing a roll call in the dark?
  Ripper: I'm memorising. Tomorrow I'm going to know everyone.
  Emmah: Tomorrow you're going to forget everyone again.
    ~ Ripper carries on whispering names until somebody throws a pillow.
  Ripper (DR): If you don't know everyone's name by day two, you've already lost. I'm not losing over names.

======== hoh ========

[hohroom] Ripper, Emmah, Chase, Hicks, Brightly, Bowie, Millie, Axel @hoh-room #st:sh.o1
    ~ The whole house troops up the stairs behind Ripper. The door opens on the HOH room.
  Emmah: Oh my god. There's a bed. A real bed.
  Chase: There's a basket. Is that a snack basket?
  Ripper: Don't touch the snack basket.
  Hicks: I'm just looking at the snack basket.
  Brightly: Open the letter! Open the letter!
    ~ Ripper opens the letter from home, reads two lines, and has to stop.
  Emmah: Oh no. Somebody hug Ripper.
    ~ Everybody hugs Ripper. It is a lot of hugs.
  Chase (DR): Everybody was crying with Ripper. Everybody was also looking at the photos on the wall, working out who Ripper cares about. Nobody misses anything in here.
    ~ Everyone leaves except one, who lingers by the door, waiting for the others to go.
  Ripper (DR): Whoever stays behind wants a private word. There's always somebody who stays behind.

[latenight] Hicks, Beth, Ripper, Owen @bedroom #st:gn2.1
    ~ Bedroom, long after lights out. A whisper from one bed sets off the others.
  Hicks: Is anyone awake?
  Beth: No.
  Ripper: Okay. Yes.
  Hicks: Do you ever think about how weird this is? Strangers, sleeping in one room, on TV?
  Ripper: Every night.
  Beth: I'm thinking about it right now. Thanks.
  Hicks: Sorry. Sorry, sorry.
  Owen: Can everyone think about it quieter?
  Ripper (DR): Every night someone asks 'is anyone awake?' Every night everyone is.

======== house ========

[formed] Beth, Emmah @backyard #st:af4.2
    ~ Scary is teaching Hicks a handshake with far too many steps.
  Emmah: I keep thinking about who I could actually trust at the end of this.
  Beth: And who did you come up with?
  Emmah: It's a really short list. It's you. That's the whole list.
  Beth: Then let's not waste it. You and me, final two.
  Emmah: Final two.
  Beth (DR): Emmah said I'm the only person he trusts in here, so we made it a final two. That's the nicest thing anyone's said to me in this house. That's one more vote I can count on, and Emmah doesn't realise I'm the one steering it.

[hohweek.plan.quiet] Ripper, Chase @hoh-room #st:hw.qu4
    ~ HOH room. Chase is reading out names from a list Ripper wrote. Ripper says no to every one of them.
  Chase: What about the big players?
  Ripper: Not this week. Every one of them has friends who'd come after me.
  Chase: So who's left?
  Ripper: Emmah and Beth. Nobody's going to start a war over them.
  Chase: Isn't that a bit of a waste?
  Ripper: Maybe. But it's safe, and safe is what I need this week.
  Ripper (DR): I'd love to make a big move. I'd also love to still be here in a month. This week I'm choosing the second one.

[hohweek.fish.honest] Ripper, Emmah @hoh-room #st:hw.fh1
    ~ HOH room. Emmah knocks, comes in, and does not sit down.
  Emmah: Just tell me. Am I going up?
  Ripper: ...Yes. I'm not going to lie to you.
  Emmah: Wow. Okay. Thank you for saying it to my face, I guess.
  Ripper: You'd have found out at the ceremony anyway. You deserved to hear it from me.
  Emmah: Is there anything I can say?
  Ripper: Win the veto.
  Emmah (DR): At least Ripper was honest. It doesn't make it hurt less. It does make me want to win that veto more than anything.
  Ripper (DR): Everyone's been up to see me before dinner. Everyone except Emmah. I've noticed.

[hohweek.float.any] Axel, Brightly @bedroom #st:hw.fo4

======== house ========

[afternoms] Emmah, Beth, Ripper, Millie, Scary, Owen, Hicks, Axel @kitchen #st:xa.2
    ~ After nominations, the kitchen. Ripper comes down and makes a sandwich in total silence.
  Ripper: Does anyone want anything while I'm here?
  Millie: No, we're okay. Thanks.
    ~ Ripper takes the sandwich back upstairs. Nobody says a word until the door closes.
  Owen: Well, that was awkward.
  Emmah: I didn't know what to say to Ripper. I still don't.
  Beth (DR): The HOH came down to make a sandwich and the whole kitchen went quiet. That's what power does in this house.
    ~ At the other end, something has started between Millie and Scary.
  Millie (DR): Scary kept me up half the night yesterday, talking and laughing after lights out. I asked twice.
  Millie: Can I just ask — why did you do that yesterday?
  Scary: Do what?
  Millie: You know what.
  Scary: I genuinely don't.
  Millie: Okay, so you're going to make me say it. Fine. You made me look stupid. In front of people I'm trying to work with.
  Scary: That wasn't— I wasn't trying to make you look like anything.
  Millie: But you did.
  Scary: Then I'm sorry it came across like that.
  Millie: 'Came across like that.' That's not sorry. That's a sorry that blames me.
  Scary: What do you want me to say?
  Millie: I want you to say you did it!
  Scary: Fine! I did it! Happy?
  Millie: ...No. Actually. Not really.
  Beth: Should we… go?
  Emmah: No. I want to see this.
  Scary (DR): Millie wanted an apology and a confession and a fight all at once. I can only do two of those at once. I hate this. I'll go and fix it with Millie tomorrow, because I don't want to live like this.
    ~ Millie storms into the kitchen holding an armful of clothes, still dripping from the hot tub.
  Ripper: Okay, that went further than I meant.
  Millie: You hid my stuff. Before a comp.
  Ripper: I didn't know there was a comp!
  Millie: There's always a comp!
  Ripper: I'm sorry. Here. It's all here.
  Millie: It's not funny, Ripper.
  Ripper: I know. I know it's not.
  Millie (DR): Pranks are great until they're on you, on a day you're already stressed.
    ~ By evening, the HOH room door has opened and closed a dozen times. The nominees count every visitor.
  Emmah (DR): Every person who goes up those stairs is either saving me or burying me. I can't tell which.

[formed] Bowie, Chase, Ripper @backyard #st:gf.4

[morningafter] Bowie, Nichelle, Emmah, Chase, Zee, Beth, Hicks, Brightly @bedroom #st:msm.4
    ~ Morning. The bedroom. Millie’s bed is stripped. The house keeps looking at it.

  Chase: Under my pillow. Don't ask me how.
    ~ Everybody laughs. It sounds a bit too loud.
  Bowie (DR): We laugh about it. We have to. Otherwise you just lie there knowing the next empty bed might be yours.
    ~ Bowie and Nichelle have ended up on the same patch of floor, laughing at the same jokes.
  Bowie: Okay. Rota. We need a rota.
  Nichelle: Nobody will follow a rota.
  Bowie: They will if it's laminated.
  Nichelle: We don't have a laminator.
  Bowie: Then they'll follow it out of respect.
  Nichelle: Respect for what?
  Bowie: For the rota!
  Nichelle (DR): Bowie made a rota. Nobody has followed the rota. Bowie still checks it every morning. It's sort of beautiful.
    ~ Not everyone is having a good time. Off to one side, Emmah and Nichelle have got into something.
  Emmah (DR): Earlier today, Nichelle left dirty dishes in the sink again, and I was the one who washed them. Again.
  Emmah: Can I say something? Since apparently everyone else gets to.
  Nichelle: Here we go.
  Emmah: No, not 'here we go'. You don't get to 'here we go' me.
  Nichelle: Okay. Say it.
  Emmah: Since earlier today, you've been rolling your eyes every time I open my mouth. Every time. At dinner, in the kitchen, in front of people—
  Nichelle: I don't roll my eyes—
  Emmah: —you're doing it right now!
  Nichelle: Because you're being dramatic!
  Emmah: I'm being dramatic? You haven't said one nice word to me since!
  Nichelle: Maybe because you haven't given me a reason to!
    ~ Nobody else in the house is talking any more.

  Nichelle: You asked.
  Emmah: Yeah. I did.
    ~ Emmah walks out.
    ~ The whole room has gone quiet. Bowie hasn’t moved in a full minute.

    ~ By lunchtime, the empty bed has been taken. Nobody admits to taking it.

[milestone] Bowie, Hicks, Ripper @kitchen
    ~ The morning after Nichelle left. The house is quiet at breakfast, and nobody says why.
  Bowie: Nichelle is on the jury now.
  Hicks: I know. The first one.
  Bowie: Which means from now on, everybody who walks out that door gets a vote on who wins.
  Hicks: So now it matters how we do it, not just who.
  Bowie: Every one of them is going to remember how they went out.
  Hicks (DR): Everything changed last night. Every person I help evict from now on is somebody I have to face at the end and ask for a vote. I can't burn anybody any more.
  Ripper (DR): I've won three competitions in here. The jury will respect that. But first I have to get to the end, and everyone here knows I'm the one to stop.

[morningafter] Chase, Bowie, Ripper, Caleb, Beth, Scary, Brightly, Hicks @kitchen #st:msm.1
    ~ The morning after. Somebody has put Nichelle's cereal bowl away. Somebody else has taken it out again.
  Chase: Who keeps getting that bowl out?
  Bowie: Me. It feels wrong to put it away.
  Caleb: Can I use it? There are no clean bowls.
  Bowie: No. Use something else.
  Beth: Here. Use a mug.
  Caleb (DR): I ate cereal out of a mug because Bowie is guarding Nichelle's bowl. Everybody misses people differently in here.
  Chase: Hey Ripper, have you ever seen Bowie try to do a push-up?
  Bowie: Don't.
  Chase: It's like watching a fish on a dock.
  Ripper: Stop, that's mean.
  Ripper: ...Can you do it again, though?
    ~ Chase does a very floppy impression. Ripper cries laughing.
  Bowie: I've done four push-ups today. That's four more than Chase's ever seen me do.
  Chase: That's the spirit!
  Bowie (DR): I'm laughing on the outside. On the inside, I'm doing push-ups out of spite, and Chase will never see them. Chase and I have voted the same way three times now. That's not an accident.
    ~ The house starts to split back into its little groups, and everybody checks which group is bigger now.
  Caleb (DR): One fewer person means every vote counts more. You can watch people working out whose side they're on.

[banter] Hicks, Chase @living-room #st:bz.7
    ~ Living room. Hicks and Chase are arguing about the best seat on the sofa.

  Hicks (DR): I'd trust Beth with my game. I would not trust myself with Beth's hair. Two very different things.

[style.perceptive] Beth, Scary @kitchen #st:gy-sr.6
    ~ The kitchen. Scary asks Beth how she always seems to know things first.

  Scary: We all listen.
  Beth: No. You all wait for your turn to talk.

[homesick] Caleb, Hicks @bedroom #st:lh.h4
    ~ Beth is buried under a duvet. Only the feet are visible.

======== veto-ceremony ========

[backyard] Bowie, Chase, Scary, Beth, Ripper, Brightly, Caleb, Hicks @backyard #st:msy.2
    ~ Backyard. Everybody is lying in the sun in a row, arguing about sun cream.
  Bowie: Has anyone got sun cream?
  Chase: There's one bottle. It's mine.
  Scary: Can I borrow it?
  Chase: It's the last of it.
  Beth: So you're just going to let us burn?
  Chase: Yes.
  Ripper: Wow. Ruthless.
  Chase: It's not ruthless, it's sun cream.
  Scary (DR): Chase wouldn't share sun cream. That tells me exactly what Chase would do with a veto.
  Chase: If you've got something to say, say it to my face.
  Bowie: Fine. Here's my face.
  Chase: Go on, then.
  Bowie: You're fake. You're nice to everyone's face and then you go and do whatever you want.
  Chase: That's rich, coming from you.
  Bowie: What's that supposed to mean?
  Chase: It means you're the fakest person in this house and everyone knows it but you.
  Bowie: Name one person who thinks that.
  Chase: I'm not going to throw people under the bus like you do.
  Bowie: Because there's nobody to throw!
  Chase: Keep telling yourself that.
  Bowie (DR): Chase said 'everyone knows it'. Everyone. I've spent all afternoon wondering who 'everyone' is.

[latenight] Chase, Scary, Ripper, Beth @kitchen #st:gn.4
    ~ Brightly opens the fridge, stares into it for a long time, and closes it again.
  Scary: Can I ask everyone something weird?
  Chase: It's three in the morning. Everything's weird.

  Ripper: Some of us.
  Chase: Which some?
  Ripper: I'm not answering that in a room full of witnesses.
  Beth: Smart.
  Scary: I think we would. All of us. We'd have a group chat.
  Chase: The group chat would be chaos.
  Scary: The best chaos.
  Beth (DR): It's sweet. It's also exactly what you say to people you're about to vote out.

[veto-left-up] Beth, Bowie @living-room #vf.p8

  Beth: It has to be.
  Beth (DR): It isn't fair enough. But what else do you say?

======== campaign ========

[pitch] Beth, Brightly, Caleb @living-room #st:cpg.o10

  Chase (DR): Twice. Caleb looked at the door twice. I'm not getting that vote.

[pitch] Beth, Bowie @bedroom #st:cpg.o1

  Bowie: I'll think about it.

[pitch] Chase, Scary @backyard #st:cpg.o30

  Chase: If you change your mind...

======== campaign ========

[vote.plan.bloc] Scary, Hicks @kitchen #st:vt.pb7

[vote.swing.yes] Caleb, Beth @living-room #st:vt.sy3

[vote.read.safe] Chase, Bowie @backyard #st:vt.rs3

  Caleb (DR): That's the saddest 'okay' I've ever said. Is it a showmance? Who knows. It's fun, and I'm not overthinking it.

[witness-after] Beth, Bowie @kitchen #st:wa.td3
  Beth: Just tell me if you're with Caleb now.
  Bowie: I'm not with anybody. I disagreed with you once.
  Beth: Once is all it takes in here.
  Bowie: Then I guess it took once.
  Beth: Wow.
  Bowie (DR): If one honest sentence is enough to lose Beth, I didn't have Beth in the first place.

[phase-veto-holder-weighs] Caleb, Bowie @bedroom #pg2.4
    ~ Hicks is on the top bunk, very still, the way people are when they are listening.
  Bowie: What are you going to do?
  Caleb: Everyone keeps asking me that.
  Bowie: Because everyone wants to know.
  Caleb: Well, I'll tell everyone at the same time. At the meeting.
  Bowie: You don't know yet, do you?
  Caleb: ...No.
  Caleb (DR): I don't know yet. I've been pretending I do, because when people think you've decided, they leave you alone.

[social-info-trade] Beth, Caleb @backyard #tg.t3
  Beth: I'll tell you about Bowie if you tell me where the vote was this morning.
  Caleb: Deal. You first.
  Beth: Bowie wants a new alliance. Without either of us.
  Caleb: ...Okay. The vote's still split. But not for long.

[showmance-two-votes] Caleb, Hicks @living-room #cv.4
    ~ Hicks pitches Caleb. Before answering, Caleb looks across the room at Bowie.
  Caleb: Let me talk to Bowie.
  Hicks: Of course you will.
  Hicks (DR): That conversation had three people in it.

[arc-endgame-unbeatable-realization] Hicks, Beth @backyard #ql.b3
  Hicks: Bowie beats me. Bowie beats you. That's the whole conversation.
  Beth: So we do something about it.
    ~ The jokes stop for a second.
  Hicks: Are we really going to fall out over Caleb and Bowie?
  Beth: Looks like it.

[deals-vote-pitch] Hicks, Caleb @storage-room #tc.l12
    ~ Storage room, between the cereal and the paper towels. Nobody else is in here, which is the point.
  Hicks: There aren't many of us left. You know who wins if I go, right?
  Caleb: Tell me who you think.
  Hicks: Whoever has the most friends on the jury. And it isn't you. Not yet.
  Caleb (DR): That got under my skin. In a good way. Maybe.

======== veto-ceremony ========

[banter] Hicks, Bowie @kitchen #st:bz.8
    ~ Kitchen. Hicks has made toast for Bowie without being asked.
  Bowie: What's this for?
  Hicks: It's toast. It isn't for anything.
  Bowie: In this house, toast is always for something.
  Hicks: Fine. It's for you not being weird about the toast.
  Bowie: ...That's fair. Thank you.
  Hicks: See? That wasn't so hard.
  Bowie (DR): I keep waiting to find out what the toast was for. I think it was just toast. In this house, that's rare.

[veto-left-up] Beth, Caleb @living-room #vf.v4
    ~ Hicks is curled at the end of the sofa with a blanket over her head, which is how she naps.
  Beth: Did you mean it when you said it?
  Caleb: I did. At the time.
  Beth: And now?
  Caleb: Now the week's different.
  Beth: The week's the same. You're different.
  Beth (DR): Caleb looked me in the eye and promised me that veto. Now I know what a promise from Caleb is worth.

======== campaign ========

[cold] Beth, Bowie @bathroom #st:sc.5
  Bowie (DR): The other day, Beth told the same story for about the sixth time, and I finally said something. Now it's a whole thing.
    ~ Bathroom. Beth and Bowie are at the mirrors, side by side, brushing their teeth. Neither of them looks sideways.
  Beth: Are you done with the toothpaste?
  Bowie: No.
    ~ Bowie puts more toothpaste on, very slowly.
  Bowie: Now I am.
  Beth (DR): That's what we are now. Two people who are petty with toothpaste.

[apology] Beth, Bowie @have-not-room #st:sp.r6
    ~ The have-not room, empty for once.
  Beth: So I wanted to clear the air about earlier today.
  Bowie: The air's clear. I know exactly what you think of me.
  Beth: That's not what I think of you.
  Bowie: Then why'd you say it?
  Beth: Because I was angry. People say stuff when they're angry.
  Bowie: People say what they mean when they're angry. The rest of the time they're just being polite.
  Beth: ...Okay. I'm still sorry.
  Bowie: Noted.
  Beth (DR): I said sorry and got 'noted', like I was a note stuck on the fridge. It stung more than I expected.

[lobby] Hicks, Caleb @kitchen #st:tl.l5
  Hicks: I hate that I have to ask you this.
  Caleb: You don't have to ask. You've got it.
  Hicks: ...What?
  Caleb: My vote. You've got it. Obviously.
  Hicks: I had a whole speech.
  Caleb: Save it for someone who needs it.
    ~ Hicks laughs, and very nearly cries.
  Hicks: Oh. Thank you.
  Caleb: Go talk to everybody else. I'm done being talked to.
  Caleb (DR): Some votes you don't need to be asked for. Hicks is one of those.

[campaign-declined] Beth @bedroom #eg2.m6
  Beth (DR): I'm not chasing votes I've already got.

======== eviction ========

[milestone] Bowie, Hicks, Caleb @living-room
    ~ The night after the last eviction. The house is almost silent: three people left, in a house built for fourteen.
  Bowie: It's just us.
  Hicks: Three people and a lot of empty beds.
  Caleb: The final HOH is in three parts. Whoever wins it picks who sits next to them on finale night.
  Bowie: And evicts the other one. Live. In front of everyone.
  Bowie (DR): I've won five competitions. I have to win the final HOH. If I win, I choose who sits next to me. If I lose, I'm probably the one who goes.
  Hicks (DR): I haven't won a competition all season, and I'm in the final three. Now I need one win, or I need whoever wins to believe I'm the easiest person to beat.
  Caleb (DR): I've won two competitions. I'm one competition away from the final two, and one bad night away from the jury.

[dr-round] Caleb, Hicks @diary-room #ea2.5
  Caleb (DR): Voting against The Syndicate was the best move I've made all season, and nobody can ever know it was me.
  Hicks (DR): I was never very good at talking to people in here. Now it's the only thing that matters, and I'm frozen.

======== jury-house ========
